Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any intervals module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 207. The projective resolution has length at most 651 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_207(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 207. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^651(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)
